# Unit 7 linguistic and owned-edition mapping packet

This packet prepares review. It records an unsigned structural mapping proposal. It does not record a qualified review, an approved edition alignment, or an install decision.

The owned-edition mapping worksheet separates observed edition facts, the proposed lesson and printed page range, unresolved edition questions, and matters that still require qualified linguistic review. ISBN, owned-copy verification, variance, reviewer identity, qualification, date, and approval stay blank. The disposition remains PENDING_CURRICULUM_REVIEWER.

Unit 7 remains in development. Its readiness is content-complete and review-pending. The Lesson 7 relationship is a proposed, unsigned mapping. It is not an approved edition alignment.

`mappingPackageDigest` binds the proposed edition-mapping facts for Unit 7: edition identity, lesson, printed page range, topic description, observation source, ISBN observation state, and the pending disposition. Reviewer identity, qualification, date, notes, correction notes, and signature stay outside that digest.

`packContentReviewDigest` is the repository aggregate of instructional curriculum. It is not an approval of this unit, and it is not part of the review-package digest.

`reviewPackageDigest` identifies this complete pending review package. It binds the ordered instructional records and the mapping-package digest. Changing a proposed page range or topic changes the mapping-package digest and therefore this package digest.

`repositorySourceDigest` binds repository and release inputs. Recording an approval later changes that repository hash. That change does not replace the signed content-review digest or the mapping-package digest.

The marker scan used by this package detects only a fixed list of known prohibited mapping-input markers. It is not comprehensive copyright detection and cannot recognize every copy or close paraphrase of protected material.

sourceRevision=1374548415
sourceRecordId=adverb-formation:most adverbs are formed by removing the adjectival suffixes "-ий" "-а" or "-е" f
originalSourceText=most adverbs are formed by removing the adjectival suffixes "-ий" "-а" or "-е" from an adjective, and replacing them with the adverbial "-о". For example, in Ukrainian, "швидкий", "гарна", and "смачне" (fast, nice, tasty) become "швидко", "гарно", and "смачно" (quickly, nicely, tastily), while in Russian, "быстрый", "хороший" and "прекрасный" (quick, good, wonderful) become "быстро", "хорошо", "прекрасно" (quickly, well, wonderfully).
shippedText=quickly

recordType=instruction

sourceRevision=1374548415
sourceRecordId=adverb-formation:most adverbs are formed by removing the adjectival suffixes "-ий" "-а" or "-е" f
originalSourceText=most adverbs are formed by removing the adjectival suffixes "-ий" "-а" or "-е" from an adjective, and replacing them with the adverbial "-о". For example, in Ukrainian, "швидкий", "гарна", and "смачне" (fast, nice, tasty) become "швидко", "гарно", and "смачно" (quickly, nicely, tastily), while in Russian, "быстрый", "хороший" and "прекрасный" (quick, good, wonderful) become "быстро", "хорошо", "прекрасно" (quickly, well, wonderfully).
shippedText="быстро", "хорошо", "прекрасно" (quickly, well, wonderfully)
transformation=selection

recordType=original-synthesis
sourceUrl=urn:russian-companion:original-research-synthesis:phase2-2026-09-29
sourceRevision=phase2-2026-09-29
sourceRecordId=phase2:bounded-manner-adverb-formation
originalSourceText=Many manner adverbs correspond to adjectives, but the correspondence is not a universal generator.
Russian adverbs do not change for gender, number, or case.
Lexical availability, stem behavior, irregular forms, and context must be checked before converting an adjective.
The documented fast pair is one admitted manner-adverb relationship, not a rule for every adjective.
The documented creative pair is a separate admitted manner-adverb relationship.
In the speaking sentence, the adverb modifies the action.
In the short-form sentence, the expression is predicative rather than a manner adverb.
This adjective has no documented manner adverb in this unit.
Adding an adverb ending to an unstudied adjective invents an unsupported form.
быстрый
быстро
творческий
творчески
Она говорит быстро.
Ей трудно.
синий
admitted fast manner-adverb relationship
admitted creative manner-adverb relationship
manner adverb modifying the action
predicative expression rather than a manner adverb
no documented manner adverb is recorded for this adjective
an unsupported invented adverb form
Which English label matches the shown fast adjective?
Which English label matches the shown creative adjective?
Which English label matches the shown speaking sentence?
Which English label matches the shown short-form sentence?
Which English label rejects an unsupported conversion of the shown adjective?
shippedText=Many manner adverbs correspond to adjectives, but the correspondence is not a universal generator.
transformation=original research synthesis
licenseId=Original app text
licenseUrl=urn:russian-companion:license:original-app-text
attribution=Russian Companion original research synthesis, phase2-2026-09-29. Bibliography is recorded on the synthesis record. No Penguin prose is included.
shareAlikeTreatment=Original research synthesis authored for this companion. It is not a CC BY-SA quotation. Application code, user data, and user recordings are outside this statement.

originalSourceText=Many manner adverbs correspond to adjectives, but the correspondence is not a universal generator.
Russian adverbs do not change for gender, number, or case.
Lexical availability, stem behavior, irregular forms, and context must be checked before converting an adjective.
The documented fast pair is one admitted manner-adverb relationship, not a rule for every adjective.
The documented creative pair is a separate admitted manner-adverb relationship.
In the speaking sentence, the adverb modifies the action.
In the short-form sentence, the expression is predicative rather than a manner adverb.
This adjective has no documented manner adverb in this unit.
Adding an adverb ending to an unstudied adjective invents an unsupported form.
